公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開
曼群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專
業生產各類氣動器材的集團 ， 公司主要從事
生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行原件 、 氣源處
理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售等業
務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售於大中華地
區，中國市場佔營收 95％，台灣佔 2％、
海外佔 3％ ， 現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動
元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC 在中國市佔
率排名第一，達 33％。

主要客戶：電子業等 
主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
獲利依舊在成長軌道上

永豐觀點 
自動化行業景氣持續回溫，伴隨公司市占率提升或推出新品，2026、2027 年獲利
依舊在成長軌道上。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1) 氣動元件 2026、2027 年仍處於上升週期，預估公
司營收皆將保持雙位數成長，此得益於中國政府政策激勵與公司市占率持續提升，
(2) 2026 年線軌市場景氣逐漸回溫，預計年底時公司稼動率可達五成，(3) 電控與
電動缸產品開始少量貢獻營收，公司將陸續推出新產品，開始累積客戶關係。評價
方面，公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，自動化行業景氣持續回溫，伴隨公司市占率提
升或推出新品 ，2026、2027 年獲利在成長軌道上 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價維持1972
元（27X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 95％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔3％，現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
2026、2027 年仍處於景氣上升週期，2026 年全年營收將成長 20%以上，2027
年亦有雙位數成長：過去氣動元件 2 年一個上升週期，2 年一個下降週期，本波
2025 年開始進入上升週期，趨勢較溫和，預計成長可超過三年。2022-2024 年行
業雙位數衰退，2025 低個位數成長、預估 2026 將高個位數成長，而 2027 年中
位數成長。公司規格持續擴增、市占率提升，公司增長比行業多 10%，公司對下
半年與明年成長的預期來自於客戶回饋、中國政府政策支持 （包括十五五計畫，除
製造業產業升級外，養老與醫護亦有著墨） ，對未來自動化有激勵作用。行業應用
別當中 ， 電子業電子行業前八月營收成長20%， 電子業中25%-30%與智慧手機有
關，主要客人在產品上市前 3-6 個月拉貨，預估客戶一般規格新手機明年上半年
上市，故公司七月與八月手機成長超過兩成，可延續至第四季，下半年電子行業也
維持 20%成長；電池行業中國政府有新政策，公司營收開始逐月下降，但年成長
仍有三成，預估明年該行業仍會成長，因客戶在海外擴充產能；汽車行業公司市占
率僅 10%-20%，市占率持續提升下，明年雙位數成長；傳統行業如食品、包裝、
工具機等受惠於政府補貼政策 ， 客戶積極擴充自動化產能，下半年仍維持雙位數成
長；太陽能行業，有機會衰退減少或轉為正增長；半導體受惠於中國國產化政策，
過去每月營收 500-600 萬元，目前1300-1400 萬元，2027 年新規格上市，4Q26
可讓客戶開始認證。 
線軌市場逐漸回溫 ， 預計年底時稼動率可達五成 ：線軌 2025、2026 年出貨量增長
20%，今年市場螺桿大幅調漲價格，線軌穩定價格，公司持續提升出貨量，目前稼
動率 40%，預估年底時可達 50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，稼動率 80%時，毛利率
40%。公司預估今年營收可達 6.5-7 億元人民幣，規劃十年後年營收可達 30 億元
人民幣。 
跨入電控與電動缸領域，已開始貢獻營收：電控系統中國市場規模 1000 億元人民
幣，但有很多不同系統，公司會挑市場規模大的產品跨入，該產品資本支出低但毛
利率高。過去幾年利用五個系列產品在去年時營收達 4 億元人民幣，主要是搭配
氣缸銷售傳感器，公司自行設計功能佳、成本低的晶片，今年將再推出3 個系列，
公司預估未來 10 年公司有機會做到 30 億元人民幣年營收。電動缸產品目前公司
積極開發 servo motor、driver，主要搭配氣動元件銷售，2028-2029 有機會直接
應用在終端產品。目前市場規模 100 億元人民幣，隨著機器人市場興起規模會再
擴大 ， 中國servo motor 產品品質穩定 、 價格很低 ， 不排除公司直接買小型motor，
規劃 2027 年下半年上市小型電動缸。 
財務預估：3Q26 公司在大陸與台灣均進行明顯的調薪，將影響該季營業利益率，
預估將低於 2Q26，預估該季 EPS 為 15.23 元；去年第四季基期較高，不過預估今
年第四季營收仍將維持正成長，公司維持全年營收成長 20%以上，營業利益率維
持 34%的指引。預估 2026 年合併營收為 437.58 億元(+27.46％YoY)，稅後淨利
118.43 億元(+40.99％YoY)，EPS 為 59.22 元。預估 2027 年合併營收為 526.51
億元(+20.32％YoY)，稅後淨利 143.59 億元(+21.24％YoY)，EPS 為 71.79 元。

營運現況與分析

上銀(2049) 
買進, TP 420 (潛在報酬 29.83%)  
買進 
Price(09/14)：323.5 
Target Price：420

上銀 26Q2 受惠於產品漲價效益顯現及 UTR 提升，單季毛利率攀
升至 34.17%。單季合併營收達 75.08 億元，QoQ 17.70% 、YoY 
26.71%。營業利益與稅後淨利分別大幅成長至 10.34 億元與 8.48
億元，帶動單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。 
展望後市， 研究部對上銀營收表現維持正向看待 。 目前終端需求持
續暢旺，產品交期維持高位，線性滑軌能見度約 4 至 5 個月，滾
珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月 。 儘管地緣政治因素推升鋼材 、 包材與潤滑
油等原物料價格 ， 但公司具備彈性定價能力且維持多元採購策略，
漲價效益足以抵銷成本上揚。伴隨26Q3 完整反映漲價產品銷售，
預估單季營收達 80.92 億元，QoQ 7.77%，YoY 34.97%，EPS 上
看 2.81 元。放眼 2026 至 2027 年，亞洲半導體需求穩固、歐洲軍
工與航太產業接棒成為新動能 ， 整體訂單需求無虞 。 預估上銀2026
年與 2027 年營收將分別達 296.90 億元與 329.36 億元 ， 維持雙位
數年增幅，YoY 分別為 22.37%與 10.93%。在營運規模提升帶動
下，預估 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 將分別達 9.28 元與 11.87 元。 
評價方面，上銀過去五年 PER 主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間。考
量公司營收與獲利將呈現雙位數成長上升趨勢，產業結構持續上
行，研究部以2027 年預估 EPS 11.87 元作為評價基礎，並給予歷
史區間中緣偏上之 35x 本益比，目標價為 420 元，給予「買進」
之投資建議。  
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
上銀科技(2049)成立於 1989 年，主要從事線性滑軌(GW)、滾
螺桿(BS)、工業機器人、機器手臂、迴轉工作台、減速機、精
密軸承及醫療設備之研究開發、設計、製造、銷售、維修及售
前售後服務，同時為全球第三大滾珠螺桿廠和線性滑軌廠，僅
次於日本 THK、NSK。公司以自有品牌 「HIWIN」 行銷全球，
海外子公司橫跨德國、美國、日本、瑞士、捷克、法國，

⚫ 漲價效益浮現+UTR 提升，26Q2 毛利率攀升至 34.17% 
上銀 26Q2 單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。自 5、6 月起，產品
漲價效益逐漸顯現，加上亞洲市場需求延續帶動交期延長；其
中 ， 機器人產品受惠於半導體需求強勁 ， 出貨量年增達 7 成。
第二季合併營收達 75.08 億元 ，QoQ 17.70%，YoY 26.71%。 
獲利表現方面，受惠於漲價效益與 UTR 提升，單季毛利率達

⚫ 預估營收獲利季增，帶動 26Q3 單季 EPS 達 2.81 元 
上銀 8 月營收 27.08 億元 ，MoM 0.98%，YoY 42.30%；1-8
月累計營收 192.77 億元，YoY 23.03% 。研究部預期，9 月
營收有望延續雙位數年增態勢，預估  26Q3 營收將達 80.92 
億元，QoQ 7.77%，YoY 34.97%。 
獲利表現方面，考量第三季將完整反映產品漲價效益，且營收
規 模 擴 張 帶 動  UTR 提 升 ， 預 估  26Q3 毛 利 率 將 達 
35.43%，季增 1.27 個百分點，年增 6.83 個百分點。預估
單季營業利益達 12.00 億元 ，QoQ 16.11%，YoY 192.40%；
稅後淨利 9.94 億元，QoQ 17.25% ，YoY 122.90% ，單季 
EPS 預估為 2.81 元。

⚫ 全球需求延續，獲利有望持續成長，估 2026 年 EPS 9.28 元 
研究部對上銀後市營收維持正向看待，係因以下：(1) 終端需
求持續暢旺，產品交期維持高位； (2) 公司具備彈性定價能
力，漲價效益足以覆蓋成本上揚；(3) 半導體產業需求穩固，
持續帶動滾珠螺桿與機器人等相關產品出貨。 
訂單能見度方面，目前整體產品交期維持高檔，線性滑軌約  
4-5 個月 ， 滾珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月 。 觀察各區域下游需求：
亞洲市場維持暢旺，其中中國受惠於電動車、電子與半導體產
業持續擴張，帶動製造設備與傳動元件需求；東南亞受惠全球
供應鏈重組，除傳統傳動元件外， 亦間接推升機器人產品需
求；台灣則持續受惠半導體與科技業資本支出，帶動螺桿與機
器人穩健成長。歐洲市場方面，儘管德國汽車工業仍顯疲態，
但軍工與航太產業已成為新成長動能，加上捷克、波蘭等中歐
製造核心需求保持穩定，有效支撐歐洲營運。

⚫ 結論與投資建議：
上銀 26Q2 受惠於產品漲價效益顯現及 UTR 提升，單季毛利
率 攀 升 至 34.17% 。 單 季 合 併 營 收 達75.08 億元， QoQ
17.70%、YoY 26.71%。營業利益與稅後淨利分別大幅成長至
10.34 億元與 8.48 億元，帶動單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。
展望後市，研究部對上銀營收表現維持正向看待。目前終端需
求持續暢旺，產品交期維持高位，線性滑軌能見度約 4 至 5
個月，滾珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月。儘管地緣政治因素推升鋼
材、包材與潤滑油等原物料價格，但公司具備彈性定價能力且
維持多元採購策略，漲價效益足以抵銷成本上揚。伴隨 26Q3
完整反映漲價產品銷售，預估單季營收達 80.92 億元，QoQ

7.77%，YoY 34.97%，EPS 上看 2.81 元。
放眼 2026 至 2027 年，亞洲半導體需求穩固、歐洲軍工與航
太產業接棒成為新動能，整體訂單需求無虞。預估上銀 2026
年與 2027 年營收將分別達 296.90 億元與 329.36 億元 ， 維持
雙位數年增幅，YoY 分別為 22.37%與 10.93%。在營運規模
提升帶動下，預估 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 將分別達 9.28 元
與 11.87 元。
評價方面 ， 上銀過去五年PER 主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間 。
考量公司營收與獲利將呈現雙位數成長上升趨勢 ， 產業結構持
續上行，研究部以2027 年預估 EPS 11.87 元作為評價基礎，
並給予歷史區間中緣偏上之 35x 本益比，目標價為 420 元，
給予「買進」之投資建議。

個股報告

順德(2351) 
功率第一、均熱突圍，電源與散熱雙引擎同時引爆 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $410 
前次投資建議：2026.08.10 
「買進」 ，目標價: $252

報告日期：2026.09.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求回溫，均熱片新案發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，上修 EPS 至
15.2 元，考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調
升至 410 元(27X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 72.6%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/9/9 收盤價： 237.50 ➢ 展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自  AI 產品線，
目前逾 60 支新產品開案，在擴產效益帶動下，預期今
年 AI 相關營收倍數增長；均熱片近期已通過 4 間大
客戶認證，新增 4 台高噸位沖床與 15 台 CNC，並
年底達 100 萬片月產能、明年中達 2,000 萬片目標，
惟現階段量小、今年挹注不大。毛利率受惠  26H1 漲
價、稼動率高檔與產品組合轉佳，加上下半年客戶加價
搶產能，上修預估。綜上，兆豐投顧上修 2026年稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4 元。 
➢ 展望 2027 年，隨單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 
以上，產業加速朝 HVDC 架構演進，順德憑藉與歐美
大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度導線架成為
主要受惠者 ， AI 導線架營收可望隨  ± 400V/800V 
HVDC 滲透率提升而續強；均熱片進度優於研究部預
期，2,000 萬片有機會於 27 年中到位，隨 NPI 專案
逐步落地，27H2 動能將顯著增長，並隨 CPU/GPU 大
尺寸擴張成為新一輪成長主軸。在組合優化與稼動率維
持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 
年營收 206.4 億元(原估 155 億元)，YoY +45.7%，
毛利率 24.1%(原估 21.3%)，年增 2.8ppt，稅後淨利 
29.4 億元，YoY +104.5%，EPS 15.2 元(原估 8.4 
元)，考量到今 明年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強
勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調升至410元(27x 
FY27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間72.6%。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967 年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，而在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始量產高功率導線架與穩壓器。
而順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散熱領域，並於 2026 年開始生產 CPO 
Switch產品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨，
預期均熱片月產能從 26年底 100萬片大幅拉升至 27年 2,000萬片(不含 NPI
產品需求規劃)，反映 AI導線架與均熱片需求大幅增長，順德於中國江蘇廠、
台灣彰化廠與南投廠同步擴產，並新購大噸位沖床，同時也在台灣與馬來西
亞找地新建廠房以因應未來幾年高壓電源與均熱片之高速成長期。

個股報告 
AI 導線架供不應求，輔以銅價上漲與客戶加價搶購，上修 26Q3 EPS至 2.3 
元 
順德(2351)公告 8月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM -0.1%/YoY +61.2%，為單
月次高，Q3達成率 69%，遠優研究部預期，未能再創高係因消費性包括 VCM
與文具類產品較上月有所衰退，不過預期在車用需求全面復甦與 AI 導線架
供不應求，Q3營收可望再創高至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY +43.6%，車
用方面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將
延續，此外 AI導線架於 Q2有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將
逐月創高，而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，同時 AI 導線架供不應求使客
戶加價搶產能，故上修毛利率至 22%(原估 20%)，季增1.3ppt，年增9.6ppt，
同步上修稅後淨利 4.4 億元，QoQ +45.6%/YoY +405.2%，EPS 2.3 元 (原
估 1.8元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 60 支新產品開案，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年
AI 相關營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求
激增與新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩
年，近期已通過 4 間大客戶認證，除新增 4 台高噸位沖床外也同步增加 15
台 CNC 設備，同時也於近期租賃新廠房以滿足年底 100 萬片月產能需求，
並於明年中達成 2,000 萬片產能目標，公司放眼價值更高 NPI，研究部認為
產業最具指標之共平面度，順德已能做至 30μm 以下，名列產業前茅，預期
將受客戶大幅青睞，更大尺寸產品用於 ASIC/CPU/GPU 均熱片有望陸續驗
證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限，預期今年對營運挹注不大。毛利
率方面：受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，且
下半年客戶加價搶產能 ，上修毛利率預估。綜上所述，兆豐投顧上修順德  
2026 年營收 142 億元，YoY +3 8.3%，毛利率 21.3%，年增 8ppt，稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4元。

AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，
EPS 15.2 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 以上，過往 48V/54V 
供電架構已不敷使用，故產業正加速朝 HVDC(高壓直流)架構演進，順德憑
藉與歐美大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度的導線架，將成為主要

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
2382 廣達電腦(Quanta)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/08           研 究 員 ：曾 智 楷(Kevin  Tseng ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 、獲利創高， EPS 7.43 元 
廣達 2Q26 營收 1.04 兆， QoQ +28.1% ，YoY +105.6% ，受 惠於
伺 服 器、 筆電 第二季 均 呈 現雙 位 數 的成長 。 產 品 組 合 部 分 ， 伺 服
器 佔 整 體 營 收 8 成 ， 其 中 ，AI 伺 服 器 對 整 體 伺 服 器 營 收 貢 獻約
75%~80% ，筆電 則佔 整體營收的 低雙位數百分比。 筆電部分，
2Q26 筆 電 出 貨 量1,150 萬 台 ，QoQ + 15% ，YoY  -5%。2Q26 筆
電 出 貨 優 於預 期 ， 主 要 由 於客 戶 新 機 種 的 拉 貨 動 能 延 續 至 第 二
季， 以及 各 品 牌 客 戶預期 記 憶 體 價 格 持 續 上 漲而 提 前 拉 貨。 而獲
利 方 面，2Q26 營 業 毛 利520 .8 億，QoQ +34.6 %。YoY + 46.6 %，
毛 利 率 5.0%，QoQ +0.2ppts，YoY -2.0ppts，營 業 利 益342 .4 億，
QoQ +48.7 %，YoY +67.8 %，營 利 率 3.3 %，QoQ +0.45 ppts，YoY 
-0.75 ppts 。公 司 表 示，高階 AI 機櫃對 公司 毛 利 的 稀 釋，隨著 產品
組合優化 影響 逐步 降 低 ， 以 及 2Q26 費 用 率 降 至 歷 史 新 低 的
1.72% ， 帶 動 營 利 率 獲 得 改 善 。2Q26 業 外 淨 利 23.2 億 ， 稅 後 淨
利 286.5 億， QoQ +35.2 %，YoY + 69.9 %， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.43
元。

二、 3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公司 公告 7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 3Q26 ， 公司 預期 AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆 ，QoQ 
+1.5 %，YoY + 112.5 %， 毛 利 率 5.1%， 營 利 率 3.3%， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

三、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50 /31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但 由 於客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ，全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
投資評等：買進 
目標價格：440 元 
     14x 2027 EPS

參考價位：347.5 元 
      10.9x 2027 EPS 
     2026/09/07 收盤

將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
2 
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率部分 ， 公司表示， 由於 下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可
維 持 在 與 上 半 年 相 當 的 水 準 。另 外 ， 在產 能 佈 局 方 面 ， 公 司上調
今年 全 年 資 本 支 出 由 原 本 的300 億 元 至 400 億 元 ， 主 要 投 向 加 州
與 泰 國 AI 伺 服 器 產 能 。2026 年底 AI 產 能 相 較2025 年 底 達 成 翻
倍 成 長 的 目 標 維 持 不 變 。此 外 ， 先 前 公 告以 197 億 元 收 購 友 達 光
電 華 亞 廠，預 計 2027 年 第 四 季 完 成 交 割 點 交， 用 於2028 年台灣
AI 伺 服 器 產 能 倍 增 的 需 求 。預 估 公 司2026 全 年 營 收3.97 兆，YoY 
+87%，毛利率 5.0% ， 營 利 率 3.2%，稅後淨利 1062 億， YoY 
+41.7%，全 年 稅 後 EPS 2 7.50 元。預 估 公 司 2027 全 年 營 收5.04
兆，YoY + 27%，毛 利 率 4.8%，營 利 率 3.1%，稅 後 淨 利1221 億，
YoY + 15.0 %， 全 年 稅 後EPS 31.63 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 440 元 
受惠 於 AI 伺 服 器 的 強 勁 需 求 ， 公 司 維 持今 年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收三位
數成長 展 望，且隨 美 系 四 大CSP 資 本 支 出 展 望 維 持 正 向，GPU 、
ASIC 伺 服 器專案 持續 放量 ，將 有 望 挹 注 公 司 營 運 持 續 增 長，對 公
司 長 期 展 望 維 持 正 向 。以公司 202 7 年 EPS 來看 ， 目 前 股 價 交 易
於 10~11 倍 本 益 比 ， 評 價 仍 具 上 修 空 間 ， 給 予 買 進 投 資 建 議 ， 目
標 價 為 440 元(14x 202 7 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
廣 達 電 腦(Quanta) 為 全 球 筆 記 型 電 腦 專 業 研 發 設 計 製 造 與 雲 端 運
算 解 決 方 案 領 導 供 應 商 ，為 全 球 前 五 大 筆 記 型 電 腦 代 工 廠 商 。 公
司 主 要 替 品 牌 業 者 從 事 代 工 服 務 ， 代 工 項 目 包 含 筆 記 型 電 腦 、 平
板 電 腦 、 智 慧 型 手 機 、 伺 服 器 、 商 用 及 家 用 智 慧 物 聯 網 裝 置 、 消
費 性 電 子 產 品 、 車 用 電 子 等 。 公 司 總 部 位 於 桃 園 龜 山 ， 生 產 基 地
包 含 桃 園 、 大 陸 上 海 、 重 慶 、 江 蘇 、 美 國 田 納 西 州 、 加 州 、 墨 西
哥 、 德 國 、 泰 國 等 。 公 司2025 年 伺 服 器 業 務(包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整 體 營 收 比 重 近80% ， 筆 電(PC) 及 其 他 業 務 佔 約20% 。202 5 年
銷 售 地 區 比 重 ： 美 國73%、 大 陸 5%、 荷 蘭 2%、 日 本 3%、 其 他
17%。 筆 記 型 電 腦 主 要 客 戶 為Apple 、HP、Dell 、 華 碩 、 宏 碁 ，
伺 服 器 客 戶 包 含Microsoft 、Google 、Amazon 、Meta ， 而 車 用 客
戶為 Tesla 、GM 。 主 要 競 爭 對 手 為 鴻 海 、 緯 創 、 和 碩 、 仁 寶 電 腦
及 英 業 達 等 。

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖 一 、 廣 達 製 造 基 地 及 全 球 據 點

資 料 來 源 ：廣 達 、 宏 遠 投 顧 彙 整

1. 四大 CSP 上修 今年 資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在 生 成 式AI 商 機 快 速 擴 張 帶 動 下，全 球 雲 端 基 礎 設 施 投 資 進 入 新
一 輪 高 峰 。 亞 馬 遜 、Alphabet 和 微 軟 認 為 其 在 先 前 的 鉅 額 投 資 已
帶 動 營 收 明 顯 成 長 ， 同 時 預 估 未 來AI 潛 在 商 機 仍 持 續 擴 大 。 其 中，
亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 已 陸 續 上 調2026 全 年 投 資 金 額，且 四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP )總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模 。 亞 馬 遜 、 微 軟 、Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億 美 元，同 時 亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全 年 資 本 支 出 金 額，2026 年 資 本 支 出預 估 將達 7,450 億
美元 。而 根據 TrendForce 最 新 產 業 研 究，全 球 主 要 雲 端 服 務 供 應
商(CSP) 為 加 速 建 置AI 基 礎 設 施 ， 資 本 支 出 預 估 於2026 年 將 年
增 98%，2027 年 再 成 長50% 至 1.3 兆 美 元 左 右，年 增 率 收 斂的 主
要 原因， 除了 反 映 高 基 期 因 素之外 ， 部 分 可 歸 咎 為鉅 額 資 本 支 出
對 四 大 CSP 財 務 衝 擊 日 益 擴 大。然 而， 在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI 、 自 研 ASIC 和 新 一 代AI 模 型 持 續 推 升 算 力 需 求 的 情 況 下 ， 預
期 整 體 CSP 投 資 金 額 將 續 創 新 高 紀 錄 ， 產 業 成 長 有 望 從 原 先 以
GPU 擴 張 為 主，轉 為 包 含AI server 、液 冷 散 熱、先 進 封 裝、高 速
互 聯 、 電 源 和 記 憶 體 等 基 礎 設 施 全 面 升 級 的 態 勢 。

公司研究報告

條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
4 
圖 二 、2024 -2027 年 全 球 九 大CSP 資 本 支 出 總 額 預 估(單位 :十 億 美 元)

資 料 來 源 ：TrendForce 、 宏 遠 投 顧 整 理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31%  
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產 業 研 究 ， 近 期 超 大 型CSP 、
Tier -2 資 料 中 心 業 者 對NVIDIA GB/VR 等 整 櫃 式AI server 方 案 的
採 購 意 願 明 顯 提 高 ， 且Google 、AWS 新 一 代 ASIC 平 台 於 2026
下 半 年 陸 續 放 量，加 上 中 系 業 者 擴 大 採 用 本 地AI 方 案 以 滿 足 雲 端
大 型 語 言 模 型(LLM ) AI 需 求 ， 促 使TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出 貨 年 成 長 幅 度，從 原 先 的28% 更 新 為 近31% 。北 美 五 大
業者 2026 年 的 資 本 支 出 多 翻 倍 提 升 ， 反 映 出 大 型CSP 廠 持 續 擴
建 AI 資 料 中 心、GPU 叢 集、液 冷 散 熱 等 新 一 代 基 礎 設 施，以 應 對
生成式 AI 及 大 型 模 型 快 速 成 長 帶 來 的 算 力 需 求 。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年 都 將 側 重 自 家TPU 晶 片 發 展 ，2027 年 出 貨 量 有
望 繼 續 高 速 成 長 。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主 力，自 研 ASIC 也 穩 定 出 貨，預 期 至2027 年 量 能 將 再 擴
大。 Meta 2026 年 主 要 採 用NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方 案，自 研 ASIC 布 局 預 計 至2027 年 將 明 顯 加 速，為 該 公 司 長 期
降低 AI 基 礎 設 施 成 本 、 提 升 推 論 效 率 的 重 要 策 略 。

3. 2Q26 PC 出貨量 年減 4.9% ，蘋果 市佔率逆勢擴張  
根據 國 際 數 據 資 訊(IDC )報 告 ，2026 年 第 二 季 全 球PC 出 貨 總 量 達
6,820 萬 台、年 減 4.9% ，主 要 由 於 記 憶 體 短 缺 持 續 推 升 零 組 件 成
本 ， 再 加 上 總 體 經 濟 環 境 不 佳 ， 導 致 終 端 需 求 與 換 機 意 願 大 幅 放
緩，整 體 拉 貨 動 能 無 力 延 續。其 中，Apple 2Q26 PC 出 貨 量 為670
萬 台 ，YoY +10.1% ， 主 要 受 惠 於MacBook Neo 帶 動 。 此 外 ， 面
對 零 組 件 短 缺 與 成 本 攀 升 逆 風 ， 蘋 果 憑 藉 龐 大 採 購 規 模 與 供 應 鏈
優 勢 穩 固 供 貨 ， 使 其 市 佔 率 逆 勢 擴 張 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公 司 公 告7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。 展 望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 期AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起 部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影 響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預 計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆， QoQ 
+1.5% ，YoY +112.5% ， 毛 利 率 5.1% ， 營 利 率 3.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

二、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50/31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但由 於 客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率 部 分 ， 公 司 表 示 ， 由 於下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可
維 持 在 與 上 半 年 相 當 的 水 準 。另 外 ， 在 產 能 佈 局 方 面 ， 公 司上調

王淑君 
Jenny.wang@ctbcsis.com 
◎研究分析標的公司為本公司關係企業 
之客戶。

盟立 (2464 TT)

重新覆蓋  
買進(Buy) 
產能擴張推升 2027 成長，機器人接棒第二曲線  
⚫ 在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高  
⚫ 半導體需求強勁 ，產能同步擴張  
⚫ 機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期  
在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高 ：盟立 2025 年營收 66.81 億
元，毛利率 18.8% ，EPS -0.8 元，近年持續投入新客戶 與 新 專 案 開 發，
為後續擴產需求預作布局。進入 2026 年後營運逐步轉強， 2Q26 營收
21.69 億元 (QoQ +16% 、YoY +7.9%)， 毛利率 22.2%，EPS 0.42 元，1H26
轉虧為盈，主要受惠先進封裝擴產，帶動 OHT、AGV 及 Stocker 等產
品成長。截至 8 月累計接單約 140 億元，其中半導體占 75% 、智能物
流 10%~15% ，年底訂單有望接近 200 億元，且 75%~80% 可望於 2027
年底前完成， 預期今年營收呈現逐月、逐季提升趨勢 。 
半導體需求強勁，產能同步擴張 ：半導體仍是盟立未來最重要的成長
核心，公司目前聚焦先進封裝、高階載板及半導體自動化三大領域，
部分客戶擴產規劃已延伸至 2028~2029 年，部分載板客戶既有擴產仍
不足以滿足需求，持續尋找新廠房與土地。此外， 公司產品技術已具
備進入 Fab 的條件 ，若後續能成功由 OSAT 及先進封裝延伸至前段
Fab ，單一客戶需求量與設備價值將進一步放大。 產品面， 2027 年底
OHT 產能目標 2,500~3,000 台，對應月產能約 250 台，較先前月產能
100 台大幅成長 ；EFEM 產能目標 3,000 台，Stocker 500~800 台，AMR 
800~1,000 台， 整體 產能 規模 將明顯 放大 。 海外 市場 也是為 下一 階段
成長動能，目前有 9.3 億元訂單於海外執行。  
機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期 ：機器人方面，盟英進度
持續 推進， 目前北美客戶已開始小量出貨，年底諧波減速機產能目標
30 萬顆以上， 2027 年有望進一步提升至 80 萬顆。現階段仍處於量產
初期，固定成本、折舊與設備投入較高，產量需達到數十萬顆以上規
模才較容易跨過損平點。機器人 2026 年仍偏向小量貢獻， 2027 年開
始觀察損平與放量進度， 2028 年後 將成為第二成長曲線。 我們預估 盟
立 2026/2027 年營收達 98.5 /146.7 億元、EPS  2.51 /6.8 元，給予 「買進」
評等，目標價 238 元(2027 年 EPSx 35 倍)。 
風險因子： 半導體客戶擴產遞延 、機器人產品競爭加劇 。

2

盟立成立於 1989 年，為台灣主要自動化 系統整合廠商之一，營運模式以「系
統整合 (SI)+ 自 有設 備 」為 核 心 ， 業務 涵 蓋半 導體 、 面 板 、物 流 及機 器人 等 領
域。近年公司策略明顯聚焦半導體自動化市場，主要產品包括空中走行式搬
送系統 (OHT) 、晶圓 /載 板 傳 送 設 備(EFEM) 、 晶 圓 儲 存 設 備(Stocker) 及 無 人 搬
運車 (AMR/AGV) 。 
生產據點方面，公司目前於竹科、銅鑼、中科及南科均設有生產基地，其中
竹科以機器人相關產品為主，銅鑼及南科負責 OHT ，中科則生產 Stocker 及
EFEM 。隨半導體客戶持續擴廠，公司近年積極進行產品規格標準化及生產流
程優化，包括 OHT 機種收斂、軌道規格統一，以及軟體平台模組化，有利後
續由專案型生產逐步轉向標準化、規模化製造，降低單位生產成本並提升交
付效率。我們認為，盟立在半導體自動化市場的核心競爭優勢，在於產品線
完整及軟硬體整合能力 。半導體廠內 OHT、EFEM、Stocker 及 AMR 之間需高
度整合，設備導入後具一定系統黏著度，其中 OHT、EFEM 與 AMR 等設備在
資訊串聯及搬運指令整合上具有較高連動性，有利盟立由單一設備進一步擴
大整體專案供應範圍。  
除半導體設備外，公司也積極布局機器人產業。旗下盟英科技主要投入諧波
減速機及關節模組，並透過 傲威科技向整機機器人領域延伸。 2026 年公司與
精確、敏實集團合資成立傲威科技，由盟立提供技術與產品、敏實集團負責
結構件，進一步強化機器人整機整合能力。  
圖表一、盟立產能配置

資料來源：盟立，中信投顧整理  
AI 伺服器、 HPC 及先進封裝需求持續成長，使全球半導體資本支出進入新一
輪擴張週期。本輪擴產範圍涵蓋晶圓代工、記憶體、先進封裝、封測及載板
等領域，同步帶動廠內自動化搬運設備需求。  
台積電 ，2026 年 Capex 已上修至 600 ～640 億美元 ，公司更宣布額外投資 1,000
億美元用以在美新建 4 座新廠 ；也規劃台灣未來數年將興建高達 13 座先進製
程及先進封裝廠房 。我們預估台積電 2026/2027/2028 年全球合計在建案量分
別達 16/16/5 座， 2026~2028 年 Capex 合計突破 2,000 億美元。

圖表二、 台積電各廠區興建時程表

資料來源：台積電，中信投顧整理  
封測產業進入擴產週期，受惠 AI 需求強勁及台積電先進封裝產能快速擴張，
部分後段封裝製程委外至 OSAT 廠，日月光、京元電、力成、矽格及南茂等
業者擴產規劃積極 。 對盟立而言 ， 封測與先進封裝產能擴張將同步帶動 OHT 、
Stocker 、AMR 等自動化搬運設備需求。  
目前盟立 OHT 於 OSAT 端已完成部分客戶認證並進入接單及專案執行階段；
前端 FAB 市場則持續推進驗證與導入 。 Stocker 已通過晶圓廠客戶驗證 ， EFEM
則受惠高階 ABF 載板客戶擴產需求，相關載板客戶建廠規劃已延伸至 2029
年。  
圖表 三、OSAT 廠擴廠進度整理

資料來源：各公司，中信投顧整理  
人形機器人目前仍處於由技術驗證走向量產的初期階段。人形機器人市場規
模 2025~2030 年 CAGR  47.1% ，2030 年市場規模估達 390 億美元，顯示中長
期產業具高度成長潛力。在人形機器人關鍵零組件中，諧波減速機主要應用
於機器人關節系統。單台人形機器人使用約 15~18 顆諧波減速機；新一代機
種可能增加至 20 餘顆。

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

### Page 6

Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
09 September 2026 Citi Research
6
Aspeed Technology
Company description
Established in 2004 and listed in 2013, Aspeed is a leading IC design
company based in Taiwan focusing on baseboard management controller
(BMC) solutions. Aspeed started the business by targeting datacenter
servers/white-box markets and has been working closely with server ODMs.
Following the acquisition of Emulex Pilot from AVGO/Broadcom in 2016,

Aspeed successfully expanded its product lines, IP pool as well as customer
base and became the largest BMC supplier with c.70% of global market share.
Other than BMC, Aspeed is also broadening its product portfolio by including
bridge IC (BIC; or mini-BMC), platform firmware resilience (PFR) solutions and
input/output (I/O) expander, aiming to maximize the dollar content it could
generate per server motherboard.
Investment strategy
We rate Aspeed shares a Buy. As a leading BMC supplier with c.70%+ global

market share, it has been riding the strong momentum underpinned by a
recovery of general-purpose servers and upticks in AI servers. We expect the
growth to continue. Apart from being in a dominant position for the supply of
upcoming GB200 server BMCs with higher dollar content and better capex
efficiency, new product launches including AST2700, PFR, I/O expander and
Cupola 360, as well as steady growth in general-purpose servers should also
act as key drivers for the company.

Valuation
We value Aspeed at NT$25,000 based on a historical mid-cycle multiple
(since 2020) of 50x 2028E EPS, as we believe it fairly reflects the company’s
earnings performance and industry dynamics, secular growth trends, and
Aspeed’s strong supply chain position, as well as robust earnings growth
amid margin expansions.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the Aspeed stock High Risk based on its
volatility over the past year, which we think is unwarranted given the

company's dominance and outlook in the BMC sector. Key downside risks to
our earnings forecasts and target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected
growth in servers; 2) slower-than-expected CPU platform migration; 3) peer
competition; and 4) limited progress in product diversification.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

### Page 9

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in SZS Aug revenues, revise down 2026E earnings by 11%, 
revise up 2027E earnings by 1%, and keep 2028E earnings largely unchanged. We revise 
down 2026E revenues mainly on lower-than-expected Aug revenues, considering longer 
time for new projects to reflect on company’s revenues/ earnings. We revise up 2027 
earnings mainly on higher revenues, reflecting our positive view on SZS as the key 
beneficiary of rising foldable phones.

Valuation: We are Buy rated on SZS with a 12-month target price of NT$300 (vs. 
previously at NT$286) on lower earnings. Our TP is based on a 21.9x 2027E P/E (vs. 
previously at 21.0x), which is derived from the updated sector correlation of 2027E P/E 
vs. 2027E-28E EPS YoY (Exhibit 8), with re-rating supported by positive view on rising 
foldable phone penetration. The 21.9x 2027E P/E is also within SZS’s trading range since 
Aug 2019.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

CIOE Key Takeaways: Expanding
the Growth Runway
We attended CIOE and see growing photonics opportunities for
semi and semi equipment companies
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Daisy Dai, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daisy.Dai@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2848-7310
Ethan Jia
Research Associate
Ethan.Jia@morganstanley.com                                                +852 3963-2287

Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Lucas Wang
Research Associate
Lucas.Wang@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2875
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
USI (601231.SS): ELSFP reinforces our confidence in USI’s optical roadmap.
EugenLight, USI’s photonics subsidiary, showcase its external laser source modules
(ELSFP) during CIOE. We believe EugenLight’s solution stands out on power

efficiency, 8 channel ELSFP around 12W, which should become increasingly
important as optical I/O scales and system-level power constraints tighten. More
importantly, we see ELSFP as a strategically important building block for the
migration toward NPO and ultimately CPO, where moving the laser source outside
the compute/package environment can improve overall system reliability. While
USI’s optical-module business has so far ramped below our earlier expectations

(link), our CIOE discussions suggest that the company has not fallen behind in
ELSFP , giving it another route to participate as architectures evolve beyond
conventional pluggables. Customer design in and production ramp are key things to
watch going forward.
ASMPT (0522.HK): Fluxless TCB addresses two key challenges in optical engine
packaging. Our key takeaway from CIOE is that optical engine  packaging faces two
critical manufacturing challenges – warpage and optical-port contamination –  both

of which could become more pronounced as OEs scale in size and complexity.
Compared with conventional mass reflow, thermo-compression bonding (TCB)
offers better control over the bonding process and can help mitigate warpage,
potentially improving assembly yield and reliability. Meanwhile, ASMPT’s fluxless
TCB solution, enabled by AOR (Atmospheric Oxide Removal), eliminates the need
for flux during bonding, thereby avoiding flux residue and reducing the risk of

contamination at sensitive optical ports. We believe these process advantages make
TCB particularly well suited for next-generation optical engine packaging and
strengthen ASMPT’s positioning as NPO/CPO architectures scale. (Continued)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
ELSFP reinforces our confidence in USI’s optical roadmap, which provides another
route to participate in optical architectures
ASMPT's Fluxless TCB could address two key challenges in OE packaging, namely
warpage and optical-port contamination
We see progress in domestic laser chips' capacity expansion and move to higher

power CW; NPO bandwidth improves; VCSEL remains active
Tencent 512-card supernode using 3.2T NPO to be deployed 1Q27; Alibaba targets
4Q26 MP of 3.2T NPO followed by mid-2027 MP of 6.4T UPO
September 13, 2026 09:41 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
 Update
2

Takeaways from other booths: Laser chip capacity expansion and higher power CW 
products were key themes, with Source Photonics outlining significant EML and CW 
capacity expansion for 2027, with its 200mW CW products targeted to support NPO in 
1Q27. Source Photonics also demonstrated an ELSFP for 6.4T application using its in-
house  CW lasers, with 8 outputs delivering ~112mW per channel. Yunling displayed 
200mW and 300mW chips, HiSilicon showcased a 400mW device, and Everbright 
launched a 400mW-at-50°C CW DFB chip. We believe customer qualification and 
sustained volume delivery remain key differentiators as product availability broadens. 
NPO optical-engine bandwidth is progressing from 3.2T to 6.4T, with exhibits from Source 
Photonics and Accelink, and to 7.2T in HiSilicon's integrated-light-source design. VCSEL 
also remains an active NPO route for short-reach applications with HiSilicon displaying a 
3.2T VCSEL NPO. 
Conference takeaways: Tencent outlined a roadmap targeting the deployment of a 512-
acceleratotor supernode using 3.2T NPO in 1Q27, followed by a feasibility assessment in 
2Q27. Alibaba's roadmap targeted 4Q26 mass production of 3.2T NPO modules and 
QFabric switches followed by 6.4T UPO production around mid-2027. Its 6.4T UPO, 
developed with Accelink, is set to be demonstrated at the Sep 2026 Apsara conference.  
Tencent and Alibaba highlighted scale-up as a key near-term opportunity for NPO, citing its 
balance of bandwidth density, power consumption, supplier openness and delivery 
readiness. Industry participants also argued that NPO could remain a viable long-term 
architecture rather than a transitional one. For scale out, pluggables may remain the 
favored solution for broad deployment by some industry participants. Huawei's roadmap 
has progressed from 50G VCSEL solution in its 384-accelerator A3 supernode to 100G 
VCSEL LPO solution for its A5 supernode up to 8,192 accelerators. HiSilicon showcased 
both 3.2T VCSEL NPO and 7.2T SiPh NPO with integrated CW sources as potential future 
architectures.
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited (“Morgan Stanley”) is currently acting as exclusive financial

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 099: 260914_gs_optical-tour

### Page 1

1. Optical module demand outlook: Companies we visited remain positive on 
optical module demand, driven by AI server racks ramp up, optical module adoption 
expansion (from scale out to scale up / across), and specification upgrade. On global 
market demand on 800G and 1.6T, demand could be at 130m to 200m; for the China 
market, overall demand could also grow at mid double digits to 50m+ units, with the 
majority 800G and 400G, and 1.6T emerging. Nevertheless, companies we visited 
with also mentioned that tight raw material supply (e.g. DSP , lasers, PCB) could 
continue to cap shipments in 2027. The positive tone echoes our positive view on 
global demand, on which we raised 800G and above optical module demand by 39% 
/ 36% in 2027E / 2028E to 144m / 171m (report link). Supply chain positive tone 
reflects further upside to our estimates. 
2. Optical module competition: Companies we visited see pricing competition 
remaining healthy, with 800G optical module pricing decline within single digits in 
2027, and 1.6T optical module price to sustain at US$700+. Managements highlight 
that optical module is not a pure assembly but with multiple SKUs and require 
suppliers’ strong R&D and timely development as compared to telecom, the product 
cycle could shorten to 1-2 years in datacom vs. 5 years in telecom. Supply chain 
management and mass production capability are also important to win orders, 
especially under the current tight supply situation and clients’ requirements of 
diversifying production sites under geopolitical tension. Innolight, as the global 
leader works with supply chain closely, including R&D collaboration, invests directly 
in their suppliers, and pre-payment and long-term agreements to secure a

### Page 2

sustainable supply through cycles.
3. Laser supply: Chinese suppliers are ramping up capacity and capability in 200G EML,
supporting the 1.6T optical module ramp up in 2027. Nevertheless, shipment also
depends on other raw material supply (e.g. DSP), and customers penetration.
Everbright‘s laser capacity prepared for 2027 is EML slightly higher than CW laser, and
within the EML majority is for 100G, with the remaining for 200G; Dongshan Precision

highlights their 200G EML is already in mass production, while volume is still small
currently given the tight supply of DSP; nevertheless, the company also secured 10m
supply of DSP in 2027, supporting their lasers and optical modules shipment ramp up.
VPEC, global leading InP epiwafer supplier, also sees improving InP substrate supply, with
steady export permission from China government, more customers bringing InP
substrate, and growing second to fourth suppliers outside of China. VPEC’s MOCVD is

also in expansion, from current 62 MOCVD to 69 MOCVD by 2Q27, and planning a new
production site in 2027.
4. CPO timeline: As a new technology, CPO will naturally take time and could face
challenges in the path of commercialization. For example, laser suppliers see thermal
challenges for 300mW / 400mW CW lasers in ELS (external laser source), and is working
on 100mW+ with PA for NPO first. Optical module suppliers also see strong 3.2T NPO
optical engine demand across China and overseas markets. Nevertheless, equipment

suppliers remain the earliest beneficiary in CPO, with the strong testing and coupling
demand reflecting in equipment suppliers’ revenues in mid 2026. For example,
Robotechnik 2Q26 revenues were up 172% QoQ, or 141% higher than our estimates. As
we highlighted in Sep (report link), we estimate CPO switch at 10k / 92k / 131k units in
2026E-28E.
5. AI spending sustainability: Management highlighted that AI is a revolution, and we
are at an early stage of infrastructure spending and application development. It will be a

long cycle for the coming 10 to 20 years, and some of their customers are planning
beyond 2030. The demand for the coming 2 to 5 years remains very strong and
management highlighted their industry enjoys short return on investment, which is
around 1 to 2 years. Management highlighted the AI spending is not only building more
AI data centers, but also includes technology migration, such as making power
consumption more efficient, making data transmission faster, etc., driving the supply

chain growth. Compared to the internet bubble in the 2000s, management sees much
more transparency between end-customers (demand) and supply chain (supply), as end
customers are involved more in supply chain product development and capacity
investment, reducing the information gap between demand and supply, and reducing
the chance of double booking. Management noted that currently every module they
produce is being deployed in data centers, reflecting real demand.

6. Photonics IC scaling up: AI drives data transmission speed and bandwidth
requirement, leading to strong demand on Photonics IC, requesting it to catch up
Electrical IC automated production and testing ecosystem in 1 to 2 years, while EIC /
semiconductors has been developed for decades. The strong demand brings
opportunities to tool suppliers, across coupling and testing, which are necessary tools to
accelerate PIC production. Robotechnik / FiconTEC describes the number of tools they

target to ship in the coming 1 year would be over 50% of what they’ve shipped in the
past 25 years, reflecting the strong demand and orders visibility.
14 September 2026   2
Goldman SachsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

7. Testing - CPO earliest beneficiary: As a new technology, CPO naturally takes time
and faces challenges in the path of commercialization; however, we identify testing
equipment suppliers as the earliest beneficiaries, considering the expensive final module
means multiple testing during the production process is necessary, given the earlier a
failure is found, the lower cost a failure is. As a result, companies we visited continue to
see Insertion 2 (double-sided wafer-level testing) as necessary, and Robotechnik /

FiconTEC launched the new generation in 3Q26, which fully complied with the global
leading foundry’s communication infrastructure.
8. Testing - shortening time with KGD (know good die) at early stage: To facilitate
photonics IC scaling up, not only are more tools needed but better tools, requiring
suppliers’ strong R&D and execution, delivering continuous specification upgrade.
Robotechnik / FiconTEC, as global leaders in PIC testing, aim to shorten the wafer-level

testing time by 50-60% next year, and target 4x faster die-level testing next year.
Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered) offers NightJar, a hyperspectral image system,
revealing AWG (arrayed waveguide grating) optical loss pathways with absolute optical
power mapping, allowing customers to KGD at Insertion 1 (PIC wafer testing) or Insertion
0 (PIC wafer early defect screening), saving failure costs.
9. Coupling - automated production becoming necessary in high-speed connection:

AI drives optical module towards high speed and high density / bandwidth, driving
demand on automated production. GMT (4573.TWO, not covered) showcased dual FA
active alignment equipment for 1.6T optical module, which could do the coupling for
both Tx and Rx simultaneously, saving 50% of coupling time compared to traditional
equipment. FitTech (6706.TW, not covered) has showcased an optical engine assembly
system, featuring passive / active alignment and customized development of coupling

algorithms, which could be used in multiple products: high-speed optical modules, ELS,
OCS, NPO, CPO, SiPh and optical chiplets. ASMPT has showcased MEGA multi-chip
bonding solution for complex multi-chip and photonics packaging applications,
combining high-precision placement with adhesive dispensing, UV fixation and 3D
inspection in one platform.
10. FAU & Laser specification upgrade: With optical networking expanding from scale
out to scale up, FAU and laser specification is also migrating. FOCI‘s FAU in 2026 to be

mainly 40 channels (starting in 3Q26) for 3.2T, followed by 80 channels (within 2026) for
6.4T, and 100 channels under R&D, per management. The company’s FAU enjoys several
customers across fabless, foundry, laser suppliers, etc. FOCI Aug revenues accelerated to
+20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in Jul), reflecting the ramp up of high-end FAU. VPEC‘s
optoelectronics revenues enjoy strong growth on the back of strong CW lasers demand
and InP substrate supply getting better. Its InP epiwafer for CW lasers is also migrating

from 70mW / 100mW (scale out) to above 100mW (scale up, NPO) and 300mW /
400mW (scale up, CPO), along with expansion from CW lasers to 100G / 200G EML,
supporting its growth ahead.
14 September 2026   3
Goldman SachsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Zhongji Innolight Co.
Valuation: Our 12-m TP for Innolight A-share of Rmb2,645 is based on 35.6x 2027E P/E,
and our 12-m TP for Innolight H-share of HK$3,267 is based on a H-A premium of 13%.
We derive our target P/E multiple from the networking peers’ trading P/E vs.
forward-year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM). Compared to Innolight’s historical trading
range, the target multiple reflects our view of its leading market position in high-speed

connections and product-mix upgrade toward SiPh optical modules, driving GM
expansion. We are Buy rated.
Key downside risks: 1) slower-/worse-than-expected 800G+ optical modules demand;
2) slower/worse-than-expected new optical devices ramp pace; 3)
faster-than-expected market share normalizing in 800G+ optical modules; 4)
geopolitical risks; and 5) worsening component supply situation to constrain shipment
growth.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC

Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is
based on a 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics
supply chain.
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - RoboTechnik
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of Rmb796 is based on a 20.7x 2031E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 11.3%. Our target multiple is derived from the
correlation between consumer electronic peers’ PEG&M (2026E P/E to 2027E NI YoY and
OPM), with RoboTechnik’s 2032E net income at 2% YoY and 2032E OPM at 32%.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition; lower-than-expected

optical connection end demand; worse-than-expected photovoltaic market downcycle.
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of NT$833 is based on a 15.4x 2030E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 12.6%. Our target multiple is derived from peers’
correlation between forward-year trading P/E and earnings growth.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition, technology migration
trends in optical networking, and slower-than-expected capacity ramp-up.

Price target methodology and risks - ASMPT
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on ASMPT with a 12-month target price
of HK$177, which is based on a 24.9x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and
OPM.
Key upside/downside risks: 1) customers’ faster-/slower-than-expected adoption of
Advanced Packaging tools; 2) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand from

automotive customers; and 3) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for traditional
IC packaging and SMT equipment.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

UMT provides rectangular waveguides, which are conducting pipes with a 
rectangular cross-section used to guide the propagation of microwave or mmwave 
signals. Different shapes and combinations of waveguides can form various RF 
passive components, such as filter / diplexer, coupler, isolator, antenna, power 
amplifiers (PAs), etc. The company serves leading global LEO satellite operators, and 
its waveguides can be seen in LEO satellites / payloads (main), and gateways in 
ground stations.

### Page 2

LEO satellite launches update: Starlink launched 261 LEO satellites in Aug 2026, or 
+13% MoM. The number of satellites per launch continues to increase from 24 satellites 
per launch in May 2026 to 26 satellites per launch in Aug 2026, and at 27 satellites per 
launch so far in Sep 2026, reaffirming our positive view on the constellation construction 
cycle. On Sep 18, SpaceX’s Starship V3 is scheduled to have the 3rd flight and the first 
Starship flight to deploy satellites (a group of Starlink satellites) into stable LEO, and 
should have the 4th flight of Starship V3 within 2026 (link). Starship V3 is a reusable 
vehicle, with LEO capacity at 100k kg, or 339% higher than Falcon 9 Block 5 (22.8kg), the 
current vehicle that Starlink is using. The vehicle migration would not only accelerate 
Starlink’s satellite launches but also release the legacy vehicle (e.g. Falcon 9 Block 5) to 
other LEO satellite operators, facilitating the overall industry’s satellite launches. For 
example, Amazon has planned 10 launches (LF-04 to LF-13) in 2029, using Falcon 9 
Block 5. Assuming 27 satellites per launch via the vehicle, the 10 missions would bring 
270 satellites (vs. 180 / 216 satellites in 2025 / 7M26 that Amazon added). Amazon is 
also increasing its satellites per launch: the upcoming pipeline shows 35-48 satellites per 
launch vs. 29-36 satellites per launch in May to July 2026, or increasing by 28% on avg. 
The acceleration of LEO satellite launches reaffirms our positive view on UMT.

Earnings revision: We factor in Aug revenues, and raise net income by 1% in 2026-28E. 
The revenue raise is to reflect our positive view on accelerating LEO satellite launches 
along with specification upgrade driving up the dollar content. Our GM is reduced to 
reflect our expectation that the larger scale would lead to lower GM with customers’ 
rising procurement power. Nevertheless, with the company’s product mix upgrade 
toward LEO satellite, the blended GM should continue to increase through 2028E.

Compared to Bloomberg consensus, our net income is in line in 2026E and 13% higher in 
2027E, reflecting our positive view on the LEO satellite constellation construction cycle, 
UMT’s dollar content increase on specification upgrade, and UMT’s leading market

timely services to customers. In Aug, the company announced investment of US$12m 
(NT$388m) in Vietnam and procured US$9.3m of equipment for its Vietnam factory, 
reflecting its continuous capacity expansion and diversification. The company also 
announced it would set up a subsidiary in the US with investment of up to US$10m 
(NT$323m), and we expect the subsidiary could support the company to serve its major 
customers in a timely manner.

### Page 4

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price based on a discounted P/E, and based 
on 2029E (methodology unchanged). We continue to derive the target P/E multiple from 
the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the satellite supply chain, and we remove outliers 
(those below 0.3x, which are mostly telecom operators who face competition from LEO 
satellite operators or having consumer electronics exposure which suffered from high 
memory costs). Our target 2029E discounted P/E multiple is at 37.0x (unchanged) which 
is based on peers’ avg. PEG&M, and UMT’s 2029-30E avg. NI YoY and OPM. We apply the 
37.0x multiple to 2029E EPS and discount it back to 2027E via a COE of 7.8% (no 
change). Our target price is at NT$2,557 (vs. NT$2,513 previously), implying 61x 2027E 
P/E, which is above the company’s average +1 stv. P/E of 42x, reflecting our positive view 
on UMT’s product mix upgrade toward LEO satellites. Maintain Buy.

### Page 5

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 37.0x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,557. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

### Page 1

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Delta Electronics
Add to APAC Key Call Buy - Taiwan Summit
reaffirms the AI Power opportunity
Capturing a robust industry TAM as the leading server power supplier
Delta affirmed a stronger growth profile as AI infrastructure now is over 50% of sales.
Our bottom-up model from our APAC Focus (link) estimates a 69% CAGR to US$91.1bn
AI server power TAM from 2025-2030 even moderating 2028-30 for hyperscale cash

flow and grid power constraints. We see a strong 2027 driven by +65% YoY accelerator
die growth, Rubin's ~60% higher rack power and AMD's move to rack-scale systems.
400V/800V becomes a larger driver in 2028-29 as rising power density outgrows low-
voltage distribution. We believe Delta is key beneficiary with its  broad grid-to-chip
portfolio helping sustain its ~40% share of a rising TAM.
Near-term supply constraints bottoming from Aug and accelerating into Q4.

Supply constraints on Power ICs have tempered Q3 growth from +15-20% QoQ to +10-
12% QoQ but should grow in Sep and accelerate to double digit QoQ in Q426 and
above seasonal Q127 (UBS +3% QoQ) into new ASIC/GPU platform ramps and more
supply. Management expects shortages to persist for at least the next several quarters
with demand accelerating faster than supply and the supply-demand gap likely
widening before narrowing but price hikes rebounding OpMs.

HVDC Adoption Appears Increasingly Inevitable.
HVDC production and shipments have begun and should be modest in 2027 before
ramping sharper in the following 1-2 years. As rack power rises from 140kW on GB300
to nearly 250kW on VR200 and potentially higher on Rubin Ultra, management expects
space and efficiency constraints to shift power systems out of racks and into side-car or
centralized architectures. Delta sees HVDC as the logical solution for power-loss,

thermal, and density challenges, with higher-voltage architectures offering multiple-
times higher value per watt than current solutions. Delta sees three hyperscalers
evaluating 400V, two adopting it for NVIDIA racks initially before converging on 800V.
Reiterate Buy and PT NT$2,750
Delta's stock has pulled back 36% since May following push-outs to NVIDIA's
architecture slowing the dedicated power rack ramp-up and elevating supply
constraints, pulling it back to 22x/17x 2026-27 EPS. We now see better entry with sales

ramping in H226, spec upgrades to ASIC power racks and Rubin at 60% higher TDP over
Blackwell and margins holding better than feared. The company should leverage its
strong product line, R&D and field support to participate in the key power architecture
transitions in the coming years moving the power stack out of the rack and also
developing emerging nascent technologies including SST for facility level power
transformation and rectification and fuel cells licensing Ceres for medium-term

opportunity to supplement the power grid constraint with alternative energy.

Company Description
Delta Electronics provides computer switching power supply solutions, as well as video display
and electronics components, for computers, smartphones, telecom and industrial products.
Founded in 1971, the firm has production bases in Taiwan, China, Thailand and India. In

2023, its power electronics, infrastructure and automation segments contributed 62%, 25%
and 13% of revenue, respectively.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target on PE multiple valuation.

Delta's businesses include SPS and components for a variety of end-applications, video
displays and data networking subsystem and system products. Any changes in these end-
markets could affect our forecasts for Delta. Investment risk could increase/decrease on any
deviation from our expectations for end-market demand and competition.
Downside risks for Delta include: severe supply chain constraints; weaker-than-expected PC/
server demand; a slower-than-expected IA recovery; and a slower ramp of 5G in 2022.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Novatek Microelectronics Corp.
2026 Taiwan Summit: PC weakness offset by
continued momentum from major U.S. customer
Q3 revenue still expected to grow 5-6% QoQ despite weaker notebook demand
Novatek attended our 2026 Taiwan Summit on Sep 14th. It reiterates Q326 revenue to
grow  5-6% QoQ, supported by seasonal strength in S/M-sized DDI driven by the iPhone
ramp. SoC revenue is expected to grow slightly QoQ, while LDDI should decline

modestly as TV demand remains flattish and PC demand weakens. Importantly,
management highlights notebook PC has seen a meaningful downward adjustment
recently. While the company had originally expected NB shipments to decline  slightly
QoQ, demand has deteriorated to over 10% decline QoQ, with the impact becoming
visible since mid-August. Nevertheless, Q326F guidance for TV, tablet and automotive
remains unchanged. Looking into Q426, management expects seasonal weakness to

continue, with softer memory-driven ASP increases and lower shipment volume likely
resulting in sequential revenue decline.
GM likely peaked in Q226 and should normalize into H226 and 2027
Management highlighted that Q226 GM expanded to 42% from 39% in Q126, mainly
driven by  favourable product mix and lower-cost of  inventory for memory. Looking
ahead, GM is expected to decline QoQ in Q326F as the inventory benefit disappears and
growth shifts toward S/M-sized DDI and LDDI, which carry lower margins than SoC.

Management expects Q426 GM to further contract QoQ, driven by less favourable mix
and softer memory-driven ASP increases. Further out, memory, foundry and OSAT costs
are expected to continue rising into 2027. While Novatek expects to pass through most
cost increases, management acknowledged that the degree and timing of pass-through
will depend on the  negotiation with its customers.
Major US customer remains the key growth driver for 2027-28
The company reiterated strong visibility from its major U.S. customer and expects

revenue to further grow in 2027. Following entry into iPhone DDI programs in 2024 and
subsequent share gains in 2025-26, Novatek expects to continue benefiting from rising
market share as new iPhone models proliferate. Into 2027-28F,  iPhone-related growth
should moderate as the revenue base becomes larger, while management expects
increasing contribution from  premium LCD panels for Macs, supported by a more visible
roadmap. Beyond the U.S. customer, image SoC remains a bright spot due to strong

surveillance demand and emerging Edge AI applications, although management
acknowledged recent growth rates are unlikely to be sustained.
Valuation: Maintain NEUTRAL with price target of NT$540
We retain NEUTRAL with PT of NT$540. While we continue to expect GM normalization
in H226E and 2027E, we believe its GM should sustain at the high-30% level, rather
than  returning to pre-COVID's low-30%.

Company Description
Novatek is a IC design company spun off from UMC's commercial product division in 1997.
The company is focusing on the analog and mixed signal IC design and leads in the display
driver IC and TV SoC market.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Novatek using a PE methodology.

Upside risks include stronger-than-expected demand for the consumer electronics end-
market, as well as higher market share for OLED DDI.
Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected technology end-market demand recovery
and slower-than-expected inventory digestion.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Powertech T echnology
2026 T aiwan Summit: FOPLP to see more
revenue contribution from 2027F
FOPLP business supported by AMD and Broadcom
PTI attended our T aiwan Summit on Sep 14th. Management noted its  FOPLP capacity in
T aiwan will be at 6K substrate/month (3K in 2026 and add another 3K in 2027 with full
ramp in 2028) with panel substrate size of 510 mm x 515 mm. Management noted  most
of the capacity in T aiwan is allocated to AMD, which it will start from the  game-console

products and then target to enter server CPU. For the Broadcom business, PTI has
previously announced a JV with Broadcom in Singapore, where it will invest US$400mn
into the JV for 30% stake, after receiving regulatory approvals in Q426. The JV will focus
on Broadcom's next generation networking products and will help it to enter into optical
communication field.  It has not disclosed the total investment amount and planned
capacity for the Singapore JV fab, but we believe the planned capacity  could be 2-3x

versus its scale in T aiwan. The JV is expected to start mass production from 2028 and PTI
will be in charged of the fab operations. Nevertheless, PTI also acknowledges that
TSMC's CoPoS will not only lead to potential competition, but could  also have some
impact on the equipment supply schedule.
Memory-backed price hike should continue
PTI's August sales of NT$8,558mn was up 3.5% MoM and 24.5% YoY. QTD sales is
tracking to 69%/68% of UBSe/Street estimates of +5%/+6% QoQ. Management noted

its  back-end loading is already at 90% and it has been selective on customer programs
for better ASP products. Management also expects back-end ASP should continue to
increase, driven primarily by higher labor costs and rising component prices. At the same
time, the pressure to lower its ASPs and  share the profit from yield improvement has
eased. Relative to peers, PTI noted it has been less aggressive in implementing price
increases, but has increased the frequency of price negotiation with its customer. As a

result, its gross margin for  memory packaging and test should continue to trend higher
in the coming quarters.
Capex will remain elevated in 2026-27F
Management reiterate its 2026F capex guidance of NT$50bn and believes its capex in
2027F will remain elevated. It aims to use its internal cash flow and bank loans to
support the capex, and remains committed to keep its  dividend payout at ~60%. Full
year  revenue for 2026F is likely to surpass its prior peak in 2022 (i.e. NT$83.9bn),

although it believes  market expectation of NT$100bn in 2026E could be too stretched.
Valuation: Retain NEUTRAL with PT of NT$260
We retain NEUTRAL on PTI with PT of NT$260, based on 17x 2027 PE. We will continue
to monitor its progress on FOPLP/CPO and price improvement sustainability.

Company Description
Powertech is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 75-80% of its sales from DRAM and
NAND packaging and testing and has been growing its logic back-end business.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Powertech: We value Powertech using PE methodology.
Upside/downside risks: 1) A stronger/weaker pricing environment; 2) slower-than expected

tech end demand and memory market recovery; and 3) faster/slower ramp of FOPLP and CPO
businesses.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Pages: 7

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? EMC (2383.TW) shares fell 7.7% and TUC (6274.TWO) shares declined
6.7% on Sep 17, vs TAIEX +0.8%. Following the sell-off, investors have questioned
whether CPO could disrupt demand for high-end CCL. The concern is that
integrating optical engines alongside the ASIC on the package substrate shortens
high-speed electrical signal paths that would otherwise traverse the printed circuit

board (PCB), potentially reducing the need for ultra-low-Dk/Df, high-speed CCL.
Our thoughts:  This is not a new debate. We previously discussed the potential
implications of CPO for high-speed CCL demand here and here.  We believe this is
particularly relevant for scale-up architectures, where CPO adoption still faces
meaningful challenges related to cost, manufacturing yield, reliability, serviceability,
and thermal management. As such, we believe the risk of a broad-based reduction in

CCL requirements across AI systems before 2028 remains limited.  We would view
CPO-driven pullbacks in CCL stocks as potential opportunities to accumulate.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Co-packaged optics (CPO) is a long-term risk, not an imminent demand
disruption.
While we agree that broader CPO adoption represents a credible longer-term risk
to premium copper-clad laminate (CCL) content, we see limited risk to the
demand outlook over 2026–28.
We see limited near-term impact on CCL demand or our earnings estimates for

EMC and TUC, leaving our premium-CCL supply-tightness thesis and constructive
fundamental view intact.
September 17, 2026 06:58 AM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

2H26 先進製程動能延續、ABU 復甦，展望正向 
預期 3Q26 大客戶先進製程需求維持強勁、SBU、DBU 新增產能且達滿載，
ABU 受惠傳產機械/工具機業需求回溫及部份產品調漲價格等，預期營收季
增 8%、毛利率在產品組合好轉、規模經濟效益下，估計季增 0.5~1 個百分
點達 40.7%、EPS 3.83 元/季增 10%。展望 4Q26 延續先進製程需求強勁，
其中鑽石碟營收逐月新高，SBU 優化產品組合及特殊晶圓佔比提升而估計毛
利率趨勢向上，推估營收季增 1%、EPS 3.96 元/季增 3%。 
先進製程有助產品內容價值提升，趨勢有利 
2Q26 ABU 、DBU、SBU 營收佔比分別為 25%、31%、43%，其中鑽石碟
來自最大客戶先進製程佔比已達 63%，N3/N2 佔比皆為 22%，並且 1.4nm
小量出貨，對鑽石碟、再生晶圓的內容價值，包括 N2 對 N3 CMP 內容價值
升 10~15%，尤其 A16 CMP 道數由 55 提升至 77 道，內容價值進一步提升
至 2~3 成。再生晶圓 N3/N2 每投片 10 萬片對再生晶圓需求約 2~3 倍，趨勢
有利後續營運。延伸 ABU、DBU 技術，公司亦投入減薄砂輪研發，主要因
應大客戶先進封裝/晶背供電、本土化策略等需求，有機會突破 Disco 壟斷局
面，惟目前仍為產品驗證階段，短期效益不大。 
DBU、SBU 供不應求，訂單能見度至 2030 年 
DBU 目前規劃產能年底至 7 萬顆，根據大客戶 N2/先進封裝產能倍增，加上
來自美系記憶體/IDM 廠潛在需求，預計 27 年產能達 9~10 萬片，中長期目
標>20 萬片。SBU 擴產提前至 2Q26 達 40 萬片滿載，新增以高階再生晶圓
為主，有助毛利率改善。 ABU 來自傳產/工具機需求回溫及子公司合併綜效，
使得營收成長>2 成、毛利率達 25%水準。元大最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 分
別為 14.23/20.85 元，上修 7%/11%，基於近幾季毛利率表現優於預期，主
要來自產品組合改善，包括先進製程佔比提升、再生晶圓產能擴充且以高階
產品為主及 ABU 復甦及等，使得毛利率優於我們先前預估 2~3 個百分點。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
預期 2H26 DBU 成長維持強勁、 SBU 新增產能效益、 ABU 需求回
溫，營收、毛利率趨勢向上。 
⧫  
展望 2026~27 年隨著主力客戶擴大 N3/N2 產能及海外廠佈局，推升鑽
石碟、再生晶圓供不應求，搭配新產能，營運具更佳的槓桿效應。 
⧫  
預估 2026/27 年 EPS 14.23/20.85 元，分別上修 7%/11%。維持買進
評等，40 倍目標本益比、2027 年 EPS 評價基礎、反映先進製程展望
佳、能見度達未來 2~3 年及佈局減薄砂輪為下一個觀察重點。
